# PacEdu English Subject Box — Class 1 Printable Worksheets (Days 1–115)

**Status:** ORIGINAL PACEDU WORKSHEET DRAFT — `PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW` — `PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION`. Not a Ministry-issued worksheet pack.

**Printing and calendar:** Each learning day has four separate pathway versions. Print only the learner's selected pathway. Confirm physical Android Chrome print preview produces one selected worksheet per page. School-approved calendar and school-local timezone govern the sequence; holidays and weekends do not auto-advance learning days.

**Learner:** ____________________ **School:** ____________________ **Teacher:** ____________________

## Day 1 — Listen and notice — Copper Worksheet

**Shared learning intention:** Myself, name and identity

**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** The learner can circle, point to, match or sort the target examples.

**Your task:** Circle, point to, match or sort the target examples. Show the response through speech, sign, gesture, AAC, drawing or writing.

**Worked example:** Teacher completes one example using this week's target, says or shows each step, then completes a second example together with learners. Select the actual word, sound, text or sentence from the current approved teaching sequence; do not introduce untaught correspondences.

**Copper pathway:** Provide adult modelling, tactile/large-print or picture supports, a small choice set, sound boxes/letter tiles, repeated oral directions and ample wait time. Accept pointing, matching, oral response, sign, AAC or dictated words. For this day, respond to the target by finding or matching one example.

**My response / working:**

____________________________________________________________________

____________________________________________________________________

____________________________________________________________________

**Evidence for teacher:** Teacher records what was recognised and which prompts were needed. Expected evidence sample: A matched/identified example and an oral or accessible response. Record the learner's actual response mode and the prompt level used.

**Quick check:** 1. Show or identify one target example. 2. Complete one new item. 3. Explain, say, point to, sign, dictate or draw how you know. Record: ☐ independently ☐ with prompt ☐ with model ☐ not yet observed.

**Official indicator mapping:** PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION — insert exact authorized outcome, source edition and page only after checking the current school/Ministry guide.

**Teacher review:** PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW

## Release gate
A qualified Fiji English teacher must review all 115 days for level suitability, progression, taught phonics/vocabulary, accessibility, local relevance and alignment with the current authorized school syllabus. Complete physical Android Chrome print testing before learner release.
